- main:src/routes/[bookSlug]/+layout.svelte:23-30 (v1.1 :24-31) and main:src/lib/utils/api.ts:26-58 (trackPageView by sendBeacon, skipped when no API URL is configured)
- R2-15 (Kornell & Bjork 2008a, abstract: dropping cards had small but consistent costs); R5-07 (Pape & Thomas 2025, German 6th graders, N = 72); R5-18 (Deci et al. 1999, abstract: completion-contingent rewards d = −0.36)

**Fix.** Write rules (1)–(4) into the reader plan as review rules for new work: every prompt skippable in one tap, nothing gated on a prompt, no completion-contingent rewards, no peer comparison; changing CLAUDE.md's design principle to match is [USER]'s edit. Hold the data rule until [USER] decides on the page-view beacon, then word it to that decision: if [USER] removes it (unset VITE_API_URL or remove trackPageView), the rule can say no learning data leave the device unless the student exports them; if [USER] makes it opt-in, the rule says so and names the opt-in; if [USER] keeps it, the rule covers stored learning data and discloses the page-view telemetry, what it carries and that the server logs user-agent and referrer, beside GoatCounter (the disclosure the teachers' data sheet in PED4-16 needs too). Check the P1 port and every prompt redesign against the list.

**Acceptance test.** The reader plan carries rules (1)–(4) and a data rule worded to [USER]'s beacon decision; today it carries none. New e2e guards, each shown to fail on a deliberate mutant before it is kept: with the recall prompt open, the next-section link works and the prompt resets on navigation (+page.svelte:111), and 'Sleppa' closes it in one tap; on the minniskort page both 'Man það' and 'Man það ekki' reveal the answer and lead to the rating row. A Playwright request log of a full section plus a flashcard session, run against a build made with the production env (a build without VITE_API_URL passes vacuously), records only the requests the written data rule allows.

**Decision for [USER].** Whether the ritstjorn page-view beacon stays: remove it (unset VITE_API_URL or remove trackPageView), make it opt-in, or keep it and disclose it. The data rule, and any data sheet for schools (PED4-16), can be written only after this choice. Changing CLAUDE.md's design principle to carry rules (1)–(4) is also [USER]'s edit.

**Not this (walked back in verification).**

- NOT: all learning data stay on the device in the deployed reader; the stores do, but a production build sends a page-view event on every [bookSlug] route change.
- NOT: nothing awards points; /greining's 'Samræmi' gives points for streaks and section visits, an engagement score rather than a completion reward (PED4-18).
- NOT: every prompt's skip reads 'Sleppa'; v1.2's pre-question dismiss reads 'Ég hugleiddi þetta — byrja að lesa' (still one tap, not gating).
- NOT: a test can press 'Merkja kafla sem lesinn' with the recall prompt open; the prompt opens only after markAsRead, and the button is then removed by {#if !isRead}.
- NOT: a request log from a CI or worktree build shows that nothing is sent; without VITE_API_URL trackEvent returns early, so the check passes vacuously.
- NOT: that the reader is settled to stay outside ePrivacy Art. 5(3) 'gaining access'; that inference was not re-assessed.

**Source findings:** PED4-20

### KEEP-02. Keep predict-first's shape: a cue-only judgment on the card front, both buttons reveal the answer, and predictedKnown is an optional field that never reaches SM-2.

**Severity:** medium · **Feature:** P0.3 · **Stage:** with the code that brings it to main · **Effort:** S · **Branches:** v1.1, v1.2 · **Also in:** Code

_Severity note:_ Medium from PED2-19 (CODE1-22 said low): the obvious merge fix for main's #249 flashcard tests, restoring tap-to-flip, would silently remove the feature.

**Problem.** On v1.1 (and the same on v1.2) the flashcard front asks 'Man það' / 'Man það ekki' before the reveal, both buttons flip the card, and the card is still rated; that is a cue-only and, on a due card, delayed judgment of learning, the most diagnostic kind (Rhodes & Tauber 2011: g = 0.93 for delayed judgments), and it avoids letting students drop cards they think they know (Kornell & Bjork 2008a). predictedKnown is an optional key spread into the review-history entry only when a prediction was given (flashcard.ts:300); it is not in studyRecords and not passed to processReview(cardId, quality, existingRecord), so SM-2 scheduling, the ease bounds and studyRecords' shape are unchanged and no migration is needed, and because card ids are random rather than section-keyed the rename migration does not apply. The merge is where this is at risk: main's #249 e2e/flashcards.spec.ts expects a card `<button>` that flips on tap, while v1.1's card is a `<div>` that flips only through the predict buttons, and at least 3 of its 5 tests fail after the merge. For P1.1, wasCorrect means quality ≥ 3, so 'Erfitt' (quality 2, a lapse in this SM-2) counts as not recalled, and v1.2's CalibrationTab already scores predictedKnown === wasCorrect, counting 'Man það' followed by 'Erfitt' as overconfident.

**Evidence.**

- v1.1:src/routes/[bookSlug]/minniskort/+page.svelte:195-221 (the card is a div; the predict row comes before the reveal) and v1.1:src/lib/components/study/ReviewPhase.svelte:117-130 (the same in guided study)
- v1.1:src/lib/stores/flashcard.ts:281-301 (processReview gets quality only; predictedKnown spread into the review entry at :300); neither branch touches srs.ts or srs.test.ts
- health_v1.1 mutations[29] survives: storing predictedKnown: true whenever defined goes unnoticed, because no test records a prediction of false
- main:e2e/flashcards.spec.ts:51-116 (role=button /^Spurning/, click to flip); semantic-drift items[5]
- R2-12 (Rhodes & Tauber 2011, abstract); R2-15 (Kornell & Bjork 2008a, abstract)

**Fix.** In the merge, adapt main's #249 flashcard tests to predict-first instead of restoring tap-to-flip, and never let 'Man það' skip the reveal or lengthen an interval. Add the guards that do not exist yet: a unit test that two reviews identical except for predictedKnown produce identical studyRecords, and one that stores a prediction of false (it kills mutation 29). When P1.1 is ported, put the calibration outcome rule in one pure function over the stored rating, next to FlashcardReviewEntry, so it can change later without a migration; whether 'Erfitt' counts as recalled is [USER]'s call under PED2-02 and must not be settled by changing the SRS quality mapping. If the adapted e2e checks focus after a prediction, expect it on the revealed answer, not on a rating button: a held or double Enter on a focused 'Aftur' rates the card 'Aftur' (CODE1-01).

**Acceptance test.** After the merge, e2e/flashcards.spec.ts passes against predict-first: the back text stays hidden until a predict button is pressed, and both 'Man það' and 'Man það ekki' lead to the rating row (on a trial merge today, at least 3 of its 5 tests fail). Unit: two reviews identical except for predictedKnown give identical studyRecords; a review with predictedKnown false stores false; srs.test.ts is unchanged by the merge and by the P1.1 port. The calibration rule's unit test documents all 8 prediction × rating combinations, with 'Erfitt' expected as [USER] decides under PED2-02.

**Not this (walked back in verification).**

- NOT: CalibrationTab has yet to consume predictedKnown; v1.2's CalibrationTab.svelte:24-36 already does, with the implicit rule predictedKnown === wasCorrect (good or easy), so writing that rule down documents it but does not settle 'Erfitt'.
- NOT: the stored shape is wholly unchanged; studyRecords is, while each reviewHistory entry gains one optional key (no migration needed).

**Source findings:** PED2-19, CODE1-22

### KEEP-03. Keep the pager's architecture: pages made by hiding blocks rather than moving them, a pure paginate(), the {#key} lifecycle and the reactivity hygiene.

**Severity:** low · **Feature:** P0.4 · **Stage:** keep · **Effort:** S · **Branches:** v1.1

_Severity note:_ Low: all three members and their verifiers rate it low. The failure path it leaves open needs an exception that recompute() has no realistic way to throw.

**Problem.** On v1.1 the pager only toggles the hidden attribute on off-page blocks and never moves, clones or inserts nodes, so every content action keeps its listeners, textAnchor's TreeWalker offsets do not shift, and highlights, the .term-en gloss span and bionic markup are unaffected (semantic-drift items[8]: no interaction found); scrolled mode is a plain restoreAll(). The break logic is a DOM-free paginate() over measured heights that terminates on every input (a 300,000-input fuzz including NaN, ±Infinity, negative and 0 px heights always returned contiguous pages covering every item), and its core rules are mutation-tested. Per-section setup comes from a {#key sectionKey} remount rather than onMount; the pager's $effect reads only `container`; timer, observer and cleanup handles live outside $state; no $derived wraps a get()-based store method; the container !== el guard holds across navigation; a re-split costs one forced layout; rollups are exempted by their toc type, not by slug; and the data-paged-section mark is present from mount, so → cannot skip a section during init. The lifecycle is sound on the happy path and on unmount but not after a failure: fail() resets flags and restores blocks while flatPages stays populated, a fail() reached from recompute() still lets init attach its listeners, and the key, swipe and hash handlers (:287-327) never check ready or failed, so a later →, Space or swipe can re-hide blocks with no page controls shown; this is code-derived, already recorded, and is CODE1-11's to fix. Two smaller details: openFirstSection in pagination.spec.ts (:14-33) clicks a hardcoded /Efnafræði/ link for 4 tests, a journey rather than a gating fixture, and the bionic comments at PagedReaderControls.svelte:351 and :481-482 are stale (CODE3-18).

**Evidence.**

- v1.1:src/lib/components/PagedReaderControls.svelte:141-168 (toggles hidden only), :492-511 (effect and cleanup), :287-327 (handlers without a ready/failed check), :366-370 (fail())
- v1.1:src/lib/utils/paginate.ts:57-118 (no imports); health_v1.1 mutations[0]–[3], [7]–[12] and [16] killed
- semantic-drift items[8] (no interaction with textAnchor, highlights, the gloss span or bionic reading) and items[22] (lifecycle)
- v1.1:e2e/pagination.spec.ts:340-357 (→ on arrival), :359-379 (rebound shortcut), :418-441 (rollups scroll); no highlight-restore e2e: 0 hits for highlight, annotation or TextHighlighter in v1.1's e2e/, and main's only hit is the rename-migration spec

**Fix.** Preserve this through the merge and the P1 port. Put new logic (the hash codec, key and swipe decisions, the height model of CODE1-18) in the pure module, as CODE3-11 proposes, and change only the hiding mechanism, making it screen-only so print shows the whole section (CODE1-03), in app.css rather than content.css, which is the cross-repo contract; Tailwind preflight forces [hidden] to display:none !important, and no print override or beforeprint hook exists. When the AbortController and the pager-state prop land (CODE1-11), keep the effect reading only `container` and let state reach the page through a callback or a bindable prop the pager writes but never reads. Give the state three values, initializing, ready and failed; keep data-paged-section on through init and ready and remove it only on failure, since keying it on 'ready' brings back the → section skip that :340-357 pins, and removing it on completion frees ← as well (CODE1-11). Check the signal after every await, including the `await tick()` at :432, and let fail() abort so the handlers detach. Write the missing highlight e2e before relying on it (it exists only as a manual QA item), keep the dated ruling comments, and add one line on the data-paged-section contract to CLAUDE.md, a technical note rather than a change to the design principle (it is already explained at keyboardShortcuts.ts:152-155 and +page.svelte:419-420).

**Acceptance test.** Fails today, passes after: in paged mode a highlight made on page 2 restores on the same words after a reload (a new e2e; today it is only a manual QA item), and after a forced fail() →, Space and a swipe change nothing (by code reading, today the handlers still act). Gates that must stay green after the merge, the lifecycle change and the CODE3-11/-12 refactors: paginate.ts still has no imports; svelte-check reports 0 errors and 0 warnings; pagination.spec.ts :181 (← and →), :224 (cross-reference), :340 and :359 (step-aside) and :418-441 (rollups) and the textAnchor unit tests pass unchanged.

**Not this (walked back in verification).**

- NOT: the lifecycle is sound in every case; it holds on the happy path and on unmount, not after fail() (the handlers never check ready or failed, and after any failure ←/→ do nothing and readDetection stays off).
- NOT: every pagination.spec fixture comes from content; openFirstSection hardcodes /Efnafræði/ and /efnafraedi-2e/ for 4 tests, a journey that does not break the CLAUDE.md fixture rule.
- NOT: a 'highlight on page 2, reload' e2e exists; it is only a manual QA item and has to be written.
- NOT: bind:this verifiably passes through null on remount; that is likely but was not traced, and the container !== el guard holds either way.
- NOT: the bionic comments at PagedReaderControls.svelte:351 and :481-482 describe live behaviour; the observer they describe never fires for bionic.

**Source findings:** CODE1-20, CODE1-21, CODE3-20

### KEEP-04. Keep the pager's reader-facing design: whole-block breaks, sub-section units with a 'Hluti … · Síða …' label, one-tap look-backs, both modes, scrolling rollups, no imposed timer, a named nav landmark, a live region and deep links that open the right page.

**Severity:** low · **Feature:** P0.4 · **Stage:** keep · **Effort:** S · **Branches:** v1.1 · **Also in:** UI

_Severity note:_ Low: both members are low. These are properties to preserve; the gaps (two label sources, layout-dependent page hashes, untested invariants) are small.

**Problem.** On v1.1, pages break only between blocks, so no sentence is split (split sentences increased rereading, Dillon 1990), and headings stay with what follows; units follow the source's sub-sections (segmenting, Rey 2019) under a persistent 'Hluti N af M · Síða X af Y' cue; going back is as cheap as going forward (Fyrri, ←/PageUp/Shift+Space, swipe right; backtracking goes with better outcomes, Yin 2019); scrolled mode restores every block; rollups scroll (ruling 2); and no timer runs unless the student starts one (self-paced reading is where the screen gap is smallest, Delgado 2018: −0.09, n.s.). For assistive technology, the pager is a `<nav>` named 'Síðuflakk' whose buttons 'Fyrri síða' and 'Næsta síða' contain their visible text, the registry chevrons are aria-hidden, the status region sits outside the {#if}, hash and element deep links open the page that holds their target, a deep-link target outranks the page's first block on a re-split, and the arrows do nothing behind an aria-modal dialog. Some support is weaker than first stated: a font-size re-split keeps an anchor (the current page's first block), not the page index; mutant [2] was caught only by a hang and the boundary mutant [4] survived; the announcement is verified only as DOM text equality, with no screen reader run and QA batch E's screen-reader items open; and the studies are of adults and university students, Mangen 2019 (a narrative on a Kindle) backing the position cue only by extrapolation. Costs to carry: hiding removes off-page blocks from the accessibility tree and from find-in-page, so a screen reader's say-all stops at each page (R4-20); the visible label (:527) and positionLabel() (:195) are separate strings; showPage scrolls to the content top, not the target, so on an oversized page a deep-link target can sit below the fold (inferred from the code); and the -p-P page index in the hash is 0-based and layout-dependent (§1.2's unit 1 is 11 pages on desktop, 12 on the phone and 6 on the tablet, ② preview), so a link shared through 'Deila kafla' opens a different page, or page 1, on another device.

**Evidence.**

- v1.1:src/lib/utils/paginate.ts:30, :57-118 (cuts only between items; keep-with-next 'h2, h3, h4'); health_v1.1 mutations[0]–[3] killed, [2] only by a hang, [4] (<= → <) survived
- v1.1:src/lib/components/PagedReaderControls.svelte:519 (nav 'Síðuflakk'), :527 and :195 (two label sources), :541-547 (status region), :252-285 and :320-327 (deep links), :339-359 (re-split anchor)
- ui-capture_v1.1 keyboardAndAria[3] (←/PageUp/Shift+Space go back), [15] (swipe right goes back), layout[15] (font-size re-split); screen measurements extras.deepLinks (desktop only)
- R1 E18 (Dillon 1990), E15 (Rey et al. 2019), E25 (Mangen 2019), E01 (Delgado 2018); R6-03 (Yin et al. 2019); R4-20 (EPUB Reading Systems 3.3 §14: no trapping at the end of pages)

**Fix.** Preserve these through the label, bar and running-head changes (UI1-05, UI1-09, UI1-21). Generate the visible label and the live announcement from one function now, not only after UI1-21, and fix the stale data-paged-reader comment at :518. Keep element anchors and #sub-U, and consider stripping -p-P from links shared by 'Deila kafla', since the page index means a different page on another device. Add a seeded property test of paginate() in Vitest (no new dependency): the ranges partition [0, n) in order, and a page exceeds the budget only when it opens with an oversized block after its headings or is a heading-overrun page, while the next page's first item, allowing for carried headings, would not have fitted; that maximality invariant is what kills the surviving mutant [4], which a partition check alone cannot. Extend the existing Fyrri e2e (pagination.spec.ts:60-62, page 2 → 1) to k > 2, with the pager in view and at 390×844. Pair 'hide, don't move' with R4-20's mitigation, a continuous-reading switch in or next to the reading region, whichever launch default [USER] chooses (open call 3), and possibly hidden='until-found' so find-in-page reaches off-page text (Tailwind's preflight already exempts that value; Chromium first, check other engines).

**Acceptance test.** A seeded Vitest property test over 10,000 random item lists (heights 1–2,000 px, random keep-with-next and atomic flags) asserts the partition and the budget/maximality invariant, and fails on health_v1.1 mutant [4], which survives today. The Fyrri e2e passes from page k > 2 with the pager in view at 390×844 and 1280×800. An e2e asserts that, for every page of a section, the status region's text equals the visible label without filtering on a literal word (today's check at :281 filters with hasText 'Hluti', so it would break if UI1-21 drops that word). The cross-reference e2e (:224) stays green.

**Not this (walked back in verification).**

- NOT: a font-size change re-splits while keeping the page index; it keeps an anchor, the first block of the current page.
- NOT: the swipe-right evidence is ui-capture keyboardAndAria[14]; that entry is about flashcards, and the swipe is [15].
- NOT: the heading and boundary mutants were all killed by assertions; [2] was caught only by a hang, and [4] (<= → <) survived.
- NOT: the section ←/→ shortcut steps aside for the nav's data-paged-reader attribute; it reads [data-paged-section] on the section wrapper, and the comment at :518 is stale.
- NOT: the live region is announced reliably; only DOM text equality is verified, with no screen reader run.
- NOT: deep links keep their target on screen; they open the right page, measured on desktop only, and e2e :224 asserts that the target is rendered and the hash set, not that it is in the viewport.
- NOT: the hash scheme is safe to share across devices; -p-P is 0-based and depends on the layout.
- NOT: the label and the announcement come from one source; they are two strings today.
- NOT: Mangen 2019 directly supports the position cue; it studied adults reading a narrative on a Kindle.
- NOT: a partition-only property test can kill the surviving boundary mutant; paginate builds contiguous ranges by construction.
- NOT: e2e :271-281 stays green whatever the label says; :281 filters the status region on 'Hluti'.

**Source findings:** PED1-19, UI1-24

### KEEP-05. Keep the recall prompt's form: typed recall after the content, one tap to skip, nothing gated on it, and an informational acknowledgement.

**Severity:** low · **Feature:** P0.2 · **Stage:** keep · **Effort:** S · **Branches:** v1.1

_Severity note:_ The verifier lowered it from medium: nothing on either branch or in the merge threatens these properties today (main's section page changes only the trailing slash, so v1.1's replacement of the celebration merges cleanly); its value is as a review rule for the redesigns.

**Problem.** On v1.1 the recall is typed, in line with the evidence that overt retrieval beats covert (Tauber et al. 2018, pooled d = 0.43, abstract level; Rivers et al. 2025 finds the gap larger for definitions); it renders inline after the content as a region named 'Upprifjun', not a modal, and today it opens when the section is completed, including through the last 'Næsta' (ruling 3), while whether it should also come per sub-section is open call 2. 'Sleppa' closes it in one tap, the navigation buttons render regardless and navigating closes it, and v1.1 replaced main's 'Vel gert!' celebration with an informational line, 'Vistað — góð upprifjun festir efnið í minni.', that dismisses itself after 1,200 ms. This matches the evidence within its population limits: non-skippable prompts limited low performers' transfer (Pape & Thomas 2025, German 6th graders, N = 72), interruptions belong at unit boundaries (Foroughi et al. 2015, university students), and informational positive feedback raises intrinsic motivation while completion-contingent rewards lower it (Deci et al. 1999: +0.33 vs −0.36, abstract). One nuance: the textarea takes focus as soon as the prompt appears (RecallPrompt.svelte:24-26), which probably pulls a reader who used the header 'Merkja sem lesið' button down to the end of the section (inferred, not run).

**Evidence.**

- v1.1:src/lib/components/RecallPrompt.svelte:48-53 (role=region 'Upprifjun'), :57 (acknowledgement), :68-75 (textarea), :79-84 ('Sleppa'), :24-26 (autofocus)
- v1.1:src/routes/[bookSlug]/kafli/[chapterSlug]/[sectionSlug]/+page.svelte:459-467 (rendered after the content), :487 (navigation renders regardless), :111 (navigation closes the prompt)
- R2 citation audit: the rationale at RecallPrompt.svelte:65-66 is 'accurate'; R2-04 (Tauber et al. 2018, abstract)
- R5-07 (Pape & Thomas 2025, fulltext), R5-09 (Foroughi et al. 2015, fulltext), R5-18 (Deci et al. 1999, abstract)

**Fix.** Keep these properties through every redesign (the compare step, an interim prompt, the pre-question port), and whichever unit [USER] chooses for the prompt (open call 2): one tap to skip, nothing gated on a prompt, no points, badges or unlocks for finishing prompts or sections, and no unprompted focus jump. Record them as review rules beside KEEP-01's; focus handling itself (returning focus on close) is CODE1-01's.

**Acceptance test.** New e2e guards, each shown to fail on a deliberate mutant before it is kept (no v1.1 e2e touches 'Sleppa' today): with the prompt open, the next-section link works, the section stays marked read and the prompt resets on navigation; 'Sleppa' closes the prompt in one tap; and pressing the header 'Merkja sem lesið' button leaves the reader's scroll position unchanged (no unprompted focus jump to the prompt). Code review rejects any completion-contingent reward.

**Not this (walked back in verification).**

- NOT: severity medium; the verifier lowered it to low, since nothing threatens these properties today.
- NOT: d = 0.43 is a definitions-specific overt-versus-covert effect; it is Tauber et al.'s pooled figure read at abstract level, which does not say which pair it compares, and the definitions point comes from Rivers et al. 2025.

**Source findings:** PED2-20

### KEEP-06. Keep the pure-module pattern and store hygiene: tested pure functions (paginate's break logic, studySession, main's sectionRenames, the cloze builder), raw ratings kept, and P1's reactive store reads and validated settings extension.

**Severity:** low · **Feature:** cross-cutting · **Stage:** keep · **Effort:** S · **Branches:** v1.1, v1.2, main

_Severity note:_ Low: all three members are low. It records a pattern to keep; the gaps are tests and two labels.

**Problem.** Rules that live in pure, tested modules are the codebase's best scaffolding, and v1.1 follows the pattern where it matters: paginate() and pageIndexForItem are DOM-free (buildUnits and itemIndexForTarget read elements but do no layout, and are tested in jsdom), studySession.ts's buildSessionPlan is pure with 27 tests and is the natural P2.2 extension point, and main's sectionRenames.ts (#258, not on v1.1) offers pure, idempotent transforms that return the same object when nothing changed, which recall can reuse as they are. The pattern is not yet met everywhere: paginate.ts has 100% line coverage yet 7 surviving mutants (the :95 page-height boundary, the :83-91 oversized-atomic branch, which changes splits only for 0 px blocks, the first-item rule, and ATOMIC_SELECTOR / KEEP_WITH_NEXT_SELECTOR, which no unit test exercises), srs.ts reads the clock (new Date() at :81 and :113), and the raw rating kept with every review allows recomputing calibration only for the last 500 reviews. v1.2's buildClozeFront is a pure function with 6 tests that returns null when it cannot build a front, and the caller then opens FlashcardModal; cards go through addDeck/addCardToDeck with FlashcardModal's 'book > chapter > section' source string, so there is no new localStorage key and nothing to migrate on a rename, and the toast timer is a plain variable cleared on destroy. v1.2's CalibrationTab reads $flashcardStore, $objectivesStore and $quizStore inside $derived, and P1.4 adds 'hyperlegible' to both the FontFamily type and its validator, though on a build without it the stored value falls back to 'serif' and the first subscribe saves that, overwriting the reader's choice. v1.2 also relabels OpenDyslexic honestly ('Sumir lesendur kjósa þetta letur' replaces main's 'Letur hannað fyrir lesblinda'), but its new Atkinson description 'Hannað fyrir hámarks læsileika' makes the same kind of unsupported claim (R4-09), and PreQuestionPrompt's use of tokens does not make its white-on-accent button pass contrast (2.92:1 light, 2.08:1 dark at 14 px, a WCAG 1.4.3 fail).

**Evidence.**

- v1.1:src/lib/utils/paginate.ts:1-8 (pure-by-design header), :155-184 (buildUnits and itemIndexForTarget take elements); health_v1.1 coverage[0]: 100% lines, 7 mutants survive (mutations[4]–[6], [13]–[15], [17])
- main:src/lib/utils/sectionRenames.ts (renameInAnnotations :161-175), reused unchanged by a scratch recall-rename test (3 of 3 passed); v1.1:src/lib/stores/flashcard.ts:63, :327 (500-review cap)
- v1.2:src/lib/utils/cloze.ts:20-52 and cloze.test.ts (6 tests; health_v1.2: 5 of 12 cloze.ts mutants killed); v1.2:src/lib/components/TextHighlighter.svelte:51-60, :452-456, :466-477
- v1.2:src/lib/components/analytics/CalibrationTab.svelte:23-25, :61-75; v1.2:src/lib/stores/settings.ts:14, :86 and storeValidation.ts:45-58; health_v1.2 mutations[16] (removing 'hyperlegible' from the validator survives)
- v1.2:src/lib/components/SettingsModal.svelte:39, :43; v1.2 screen measurements a_prequestion.contrast 2.92:1 / 2.08:1; R4-09

**Fix.** Keep the pattern and extend it: every rule the port and P2 add (CODE3-07 calibration, CODE3-08 pre-question gate, CODE3-09 cloze text pipeline, CODE3-11 pager model, CODE3-13 open-call rules, CODE3-16 outcome rule) goes into such a module with its own tests, and the .svelte file only renders and wires. Hold paginate.ts to that bar first: blocks that sum exactly to the page height kill [4], a jsdom unit test of the el.matches(ATOMIC_SELECTOR / KEEP_WITH_NEXT_SELECTOR) classification kills [13]–[15], headings taller than the page kill [17], and [5]/[6] differ only for 0 px blocks, so pin that case or record them as equivalent. Pass `now` into new calibration and recall-due helpers so tests need no fake timers; if srs.ts is given the same parameter, default it to the clock and leave srs.test.ts's expectations unchanged. After the merge, give recall a renameSections() built on main's transforms and a line in migrateRenamedSections(). When rewriting the cloze extraction (CODE2-01), keep cloze.ts pure with its modal fallback and put the DOM walk in a separate helper that skips .term-en and math.assistive-mathml, as textAnchor filters .term-en. In the port, keep the $store-in-$derived reads in the callers of extracted calibration functions, add a settings.test.ts round-trip for 'hyperlegible', keep the OpenDyslexic relabel, reword the Atkinson description to a design property such as distinct letterforms (native-speaker check), fix the PreQuestionPrompt button through the token-level accent-foreground fix (CODE2-17), and prefer a plain analytics/types.ts for TabId over the .svelte re-export (CODE-29).

**Acceptance test.** Fails today, passes after: re-running health_v1.1's paginate mutants, [4], [13]–[15] and [17] are killed by paginate.test.ts alone (all survive today), with [5]/[6] pinned or recorded as equivalent; on the port branch settings.test.ts round-trips fontFamily 'hyperlegible' through localStorage and kills health_v1.2 mutations[16]. Gates that must hold after the port: `git grep -nE 'type TabId *='` finds one definition; the OpenDyslexic description string is unchanged; cloze.ts imports nothing from the DOM or the stores and selecting a whole short sentence still opens FlashcardModal (case C5); and for each P1 or P2 change a mutation of any rule in its new module is killed by that module's own unit test, without e2e.

**Not this (walked back in verification).**

- NOT: paginate.ts keeps buildUnits DOM-free; only paginate() and pageIndexForItem are, while buildUnits and itemIndexForTarget take elements (layout-free, tested in jsdom).
- NOT: paginate.ts meets the bar for new modules; it has 100% line coverage but 7 surviving mutants.
- NOT: srs.ts is pure; it reads the clock at :81 and :113.
- NOT: raw ratings allow recomputation over the whole history; only the last 500 reviews are kept.
- NOT: recall can reuse sectionRenames on v1.1; the module is main-only (#258), and after the merge recall still needs a renameSections() and a line in migrateRenamedSections().
- NOT: c493bd2 removed a duplicated TabId that had already drifted; the two definitions were byte-identical at c493bd2^, and the duplicate was a deliberate workaround (9fa3501) that the re-export undoes.
- NOT: a stored 'hyperlegible' merely degrades to the default on a build without it; the first subscribe saves 'serif', overwriting the reader's choice.
- NOT: PreQuestionPrompt's design tokens mean its contrast passes; its button measures 2.92:1 light and 2.08:1 dark.
- NOT: P1.4's font labels are all honest; 'Hannað fyrir hámarks læsileika' makes an outcome claim that no study supports.
- NOT: `git grep -n 'type TabId'` counts definitions; it also matches the import in greining/+page.svelte.

**Source findings:** CODE3-19, CODE2-19, CODE2-20

### KEEP-07. Keep the design-system assets the new components already use: tokens, registry icons, phone-sized targets, Icelandic control names and the Settings dialog's focus handling.

**Severity:** low · **Feature:** cross-cutting · **Stage:** keep · **Effort:** S · **Branches:** main, v1.1, v1.2 · **Also in:** Code

_Severity note:_ All three verifiers rated it low: a guardrail for the UI fixes, with no defect of its own beyond the exceptions it names.

**Problem.** Worth keeping, as measured on v1.1: PagedReaderControls uses only colour tokens (:550-601), with amber for interaction and no blue; the two predict buttons share one class, so they carry equal weight; icons come from the shared registry (decorative and aria-hidden by default, a label prop for icon-only controls); nothing overflows horizontally (maxScrollWidth equals the viewport in all 18 paged runs); and phone targets are at least 44 px (pager 84×44 and 91×44, predict 175×50, Lestrarstilling 151×44, recall 70×56 and 115×56), although Næsta and Fyrri are partly covered by the floating timer pill or tools button on some pages. The pager keeps its live region outside `{#if}`, and the Settings dialog, which comes from main, traps focus, focuses 'Loka' on open and returns focus to 'Stillingar' on Escape (measured at runtime; FlashcardModal was checked from code only). From v1.2, the pre-question is a labelled 'Forspurning' region with an h3 heading, and Kvörðun's empty state explains how its data builds up and compares the learner only with themselves. Not to keep or copy: white text on amber (`text-white`: 2.92:1 light and 2.08:1 dark on 'Vista upprifjun', the active Lestrarstilling and Línubreidd segments and the pre-question button, failing SC 1.4.3), RecallPrompt's `placeholder-gray-400` (2.37:1) and the light-theme focus ring (2.66:1 on the pager, 2.71:1 in the recall prompt, under the 3:1 of SC 1.4.11); and app.css:427, the only `*:focus-visible` rule, beats the 11 `focus:outline-none` utilities but is not the single source of focus styling, because `.glossary-term:focus { outline: none }` (app.css:1240-1245) and four scoped `:focus` rules on search inputs and the feedback form remove the ring.

**Evidence.**

- v1.1:src/lib/components/PagedReaderControls.svelte:519-547 (live region outside `{#if}`), :550-601 (tokens only)
- ui-capture_v1.1 targets[0]-[18], layout[0]: phone targets of 44 px or more; maxScrollWidth equals the viewport in 18 of 18 paged runs; Næsta partly covered on some pages (targets[1])
- ui-capture_v1.1 contrast[9], [20]-[21], [25], [31]: light ring 2.66:1 (pager) and 2.71:1 (recall); 'Vista upprifjun' 2.92:1 light / 2.08:1 dark; placeholder 2.37:1
- v1.1:src/app.css:427 (only `*:focus-visible`) and :1240-1245 (`.glossary-term:focus` outline none); main:src/lib/components/icons.ts:117-123 (grid-2x2, circle-help, target, rectangle-ellipsis registered)
- v1.2 measurements d_settings_typography.keyboard (focus on 'Loka' on open, back on 'Stillingar' after Escape); v1.2:src/lib/components/analytics/CalibrationTab.svelte:115-121 (self-referenced empty state)

**Fix.** Build the contrast, focus and announcer fixes (UI-02, UI-07, UI-01) on these assets rather than beside them. Use the intervention glyphs the registry already holds (icons.ts:117-123) instead of inline SVGs, for example circle-help in the ported PreQuestionPrompt; for Kvörðun use the reserved grid-2x2 rather than registering 'scale' when resolving the P1.1 conflict in AnalyticsTabs (circle-help is shared with /feedback and /for-teachers, not reserved). Keep the 44 px phone minimum, the equal-weight predict buttons, the existing dialog implementation and the pager's live-region pattern (copy it into any shared announcer), and keep Kvörðun self-referenced, with no class averages or peer data (R5: 'No social comparison anywhere'). Fix white-on-amber and the ring in the tokens and the components together: token values alone cannot do it, because `text-white` is hard-coded in 15 lines across 11 files on v1.1 and the unused --header-banner-text is #ffffff in the light theme. Add an --on-accent token (#16161c gives 6.18:1 on #c78c20 and 8.66:1 on #e8a838) and a --focus-ring token, swap `text-white` for the token, and delete the `outline: none` overrides (app.css:1240-1245 and the four scoped rules) so app.css:427 really is the single source. Whether the pre-question's deterministic pick survives depends on the pre-question's form, which is not settled here (R2 suggests questions authored in efni; R5 says the acknowledge-only card showed no benefit at scale; see PED-07); if it survives, key it on the module id (PED-06). The end-of-section re-ask it was meant to serve exists in neither the code nor the plan. The popup labels 'Yfirstrika með gulur/blár/…' (nominative after 'með', which takes the dative) and 'Valmoguleikar yfirstrokunar' (for 'Valmöguleikar yfirstrikunar') are pre-existing on main; fix them with a native-speaker check.

**Acceptance test.** After the merge and the P1 port: (1) a grep of PagedReaderControls.svelte, RecallPrompt.svelte, PreQuestionPrompt.svelte and the Lestrarstilling block of SettingsModal.svelte for hex colours, blue utilities and Tailwind palette colour utilities (`text-white`, `*-gray-*`, `bg-emerald-*` and similar) finds nothing (today: RecallPrompt.svelte:74 `placeholder-gray-400`, RecallPrompt.svelte:88 and SettingsModal.svelte:315 `text-white`, v1.2 PreQuestionPrompt.svelte:54 `text-white`); (2) `grep -c '<svg'` returns 0 in PreQuestionPrompt.svelte, SelectionPopup.svelte and AnalyticsTabs.svelte (v1.2's P1 commits add inline SVGs, among them PreQuestionPrompt.svelte:40; v1.1 has none in the last two); (3) src/app.css has exactly one `*:focus-visible` rule and no `outline: none` on .glossary-term or the scoped inputs; (4) the target-size check stays at 44 px or more at 390×844; (5) a new e2e test opens Settings, finds focus on 'Loka', keeps Tab inside the dialog and returns focus to 'Stillingar' on Escape (no such test exists on v1.1 or main; the only Escape test is search.spec.ts:43); (6) Kvörðun shows no aggregate or peer value.

**Not this (walked back in verification).**

- NOT: the new components use colour tokens only. RecallPrompt has `placeholder-gray-400` (:74, 2.37:1) and `text-white` on amber (:88, 2.92:1 light / 2.08:1 dark); the Lestrarstilling segment (SettingsModal.svelte:315) and the pre-question button use `text-white` too.
- NOT: the focus ring measures 8.66:1. That is the dark theme; in the default light theme it is 2.66:1 on the pager and 2.71:1 in the recall prompt, below 3:1.
- NOT: app.css:427 is the single source of focus styling. `.glossary-term:focus { outline: none }` (app.css:1240-1245) and four scoped `:focus` rules override it.
- NOT: circle-help is reserved for the pre-question. It is already used on /feedback and /for-teachers; only grid-2x2 for calibration is documented as reserved.
- NOT: the intervention glyphs need registering. They are already registered (icons.ts:117-123); use them.
- NOT: --header-banner-text can be reused as --on-accent as it is. Its light value is #ffffff (2.92:1 on #c78c20); the light value has to change too.
- NOT: change token values rather than component styles. White-on-amber is a hard-coded `text-white` utility in 15 lines across 11 files on v1.1, so tokens alone cannot fix it.
- NOT: every popup control has an Icelandic aria-label and title. 'Loka' has an aria-label only, the colour labels use the nominative after 'með', and the popup's own label is misspelt (all pre-existing on main, not checked by a native speaker).
- NOT: the cloze fallback is universal. A whole sentence with its end mark followed by another sentence saves a nonsense card silently, with no notice (UI-27).
- NOT: SettingsModal and FlashcardModal were both verified as correct dialogs at runtime. Only SettingsModal was; FlashcardModal from code only.
- NOT: the Settings focus handling and the popup labels are P1 work. They come from main.
- NOT: carry the deterministic pick into the end-of-section re-ask. No such re-ask exists in the code or the plan (the P1.2 row, plan:57, is the pre-question on section load only).
- NOT: an existing theme-settings.spec.ts focus-trap and Escape test. None exists on v1.1 or main; the only Escape test is search.spec.ts:43 (main :40), for the search modal.

**Source findings:** UI1-25, UI2-20, UI3-18

### KEEP-08. Keep worked solutions visible with only the Check-your-learning answer hidden, OpenStax's example-then-problem order, and answers paired by data.

**Severity:** low · **Feature:** cross-cutting · **Stage:** keep · **Effort:** S · **Branches:** main, v1.1

_Severity note:_ The verifier lowered it from the reviewer's high to low: nothing on either branch threatens these properties, so only one cheap test is needed.

**Problem.** On main, practiceReveal hides only aside.check-knowledge-answer, selected by class since #254 (87f5121), and leaves every worked solution visible, which main's practiceReveal.test.ts:60 pins. v1.1 still also matches a .note-default aside headed 'Svar:' by its label text (practiceReveal.ts:44-49, :240-243), but it has not touched the file since the merge base, so merging main brings in the class-only version cleanly. answerLinks pairs each exercise with its answer by data: the exercise id is data-problem-id, id or data-exercise-id (:241), data-has-answer decides whether 'Sjá svar' appears (:254-256, with a parity fallback only when the attribute is missing) and the answer side reads data-exercise-id or id (:298); all 1,736 div.eoc-exercise in the content copy carry the attribute (873 true), and rollups always scroll (ruling 2), so the exercise lists stay usable. P0.4 keeps .example and .equation atomic (paginate.ts:12-27), so a problem is never split from its solution across pages. This fits the worked-example evidence: g = 0.48 against problem-solving or business-as-usual controls in mathematics, better results for correct examples alone than for mixed or incorrect ones, and smaller effects when self-explanation prompts were added to examples (R3-01, R3-04), with example-then-problem order suited to novices (R3-02).

**Evidence.**

- main:src/lib/actions/practiceReveal.ts:228 (aside.check-knowledge-answer, by class); main:src/lib/actions/practiceReveal.test.ts:60 ('leaves the worked solution and non-answer notes visible')
- v1.1:src/lib/actions/practiceReveal.ts:44-49, :240-243 ('Svar:' label-text match; file unchanged since merge base fcf2ba7)
- v1.1:src/lib/actions/answerLinks.ts:241, :254-256, :298 (pairing by data); :1-14 is a stale header comment
- content copy: 1,736 div.eoc-exercise, all carrying data-has-answer, 873 of them true
- R3-01 (Barbieri 2023: g = 0.48; self-explanation prompts β = −0.24), R3-02 (van Gog 2011), R3-04 (Bisra 2018), R3-17 (interleave practice, not reading)

**Fix.** A guardrail, not a fix. When merging and porting: take main's class-only selection and never select by label text; do not add a generic 'útskýrðu þetta skref' prompt to every example; do not make worked examples problem-first gates by default (PED3-07, in PED-07, proposes that as an experiment); keep the reading flow in book order and interleave only practice (R3-17). Add one cheap test: through buildUnits on a real aside.example fixture, assert that ATOMIC_SELECTOR keeps .example and .equation whole. Any break inside .example is decided under PED-02 after its own measurement, not pre-approved here; if one is adopted, change this test in the same commit to pin exactly the boundaries it allows, never inside a .equation. Correct answerLinks.ts:1-14, which still describes the old markdown ':::practice-problem' syntax.

**Acceptance test.** After main is merged into v1.1, no label-text match remains in src/lib/actions/practiceReveal.ts: the .note-default 'Svar:' fallback at v1.1 :44-49 and :240-243 is gone (main keeps only a comment at :12 recording its removal, so grep for the matcher, not the word), and practiceReveal.test.ts still asserts that the worked solution stays visible and only the check-knowledge answer is hidden. A new buildUnits test over a real aside.example fixture fails when .example or .equation is removed from ATOMIC_SELECTOR (mutants of the atomic rule survive v1.1's tests today, which cover atomic blocks only abstractly).

**Not this (walked back in verification).**

- NOT: class-only selection is already on v1.1. It is main's (#254); v1.1 keeps the 'Svar:' label-text match until main is merged.
- NOT: answerLinks.ts:1-14 is the pairing code. It is a stale header comment in the old ':::practice-problem' syntax; the pairing is at :241, :254-256 and :298.
- NOT: g = 0.48 against problem solving in general. It is against problem-solving or business-as-usual controls, in mathematics only.
- NOT: a paginate test already pins 'no boundary between an example's problem and its Lausn'. v1.1's paginate tests cover atomic blocks abstractly; none targets .example, .equation or a 'Lausn' boundary.
- NOT: the single break before the check-your-learning part (PED3-01) is pre-approved. It waits on its own measurement (PED-02).

**Source findings:** PED3-08

### KEEP-09. Keep P1.4's self-hosted Atkinson with full Icelandic coverage, the move away from benefit claims, and an explicit theme choice that always wins.

**Severity:** low · **Feature:** P1.4 · **Stage:** keep · **Effort:** S · **Branches:** v1.2, v1.1, main · **Also in:** Pedagogy

_Severity note:_ Rated low by the verifier: a port guardrail; the theme defect it touches is UI-22's.

**Problem.** v1.2's P1.4 ships Atkinson Hyperlegible self-hosted: four @font-face rules (v1.2 app.css:258-286) load `/fonts/AtkinsonHyperlegible-*.woff2` (97,040 B in all), and the capture's network log shows no font request leaving the origin. All four faces carry the 20 Icelandic letters and the „ “ quotes (an independent cmap parse of each WOFF2; the name table gives v1.006, 'Copyright 2020 Braille Institute of America, Inc.' and OFL 1.1). The OpenDyslexic relabel drops 'Letur hannað fyrir lesblinda', which main (:39) and v1.1 (:41) still carry, so it has to be ported too; whether its replacement wording still overclaims is CODE-12's (UI2-10). What is worth keeping on the theme is that an explicit choice is never overridden by the OS preference (a stored light theme with the OS in dark mode stays light after hydration). v1.2's implementation of it is not worth keeping: settings.ts:108-113 seeds the OS-derived theme on a first visit and the subscriber (:117-124) saves it at once, so a later OS switch never takes effect (same profile, OS switched to light, reload: still dark), and with no theme script in app.html on any branch a stored dark theme paints light first.

**Evidence.**

- v1.2:src/app.css:258-286 (four @font-face rules for `/fonts/AtkinsonHyperlegible-*.woff2`; 23,196 + 23,776 + 24,908 + 25,160 = 97,040 B)
- WOFF2 cmap parse of all four faces: 20 of 20 Icelandic letters and 11 of 11 of „“”‘’‚–—…·•; capture network log d_settings_typography.fonts: only `/fonts/*` requests
- v1.2:src/lib/components/SettingsModal.svelte:39-43 against main:src/lib/components/SettingsModal.svelte:39 and v1.1 :41 ('Letur hannað fyrir lesblinda')
- v1.2 measurements d_settings_typography.firstVisit: 'saved light, OS dark' gives htmlClass ''; 'same profile, OS switched to light, reload' stays dark; v1.2:src/lib/stores/settings.ts:108-113, :117-124
- v1.2:vite.config.ts:21 (precache glob includes woff2); v1.2 static/fonts has neither OFL.txt nor LICENSES.md

**Fix.** Port the self-hosting, the per-setting @font-face and the OpenDyslexic relabel. Port the explicit-choice rule together with UI-22's fix (UI2-14), not v1.2's settings.ts verbatim: theme light | dark | system with 'Fylgja kerfi' as the default and a live matchMedia listener, persist only explicit choices, and add an inline pre-paint script in app.html. The licence step is mandatory under the house rule (CODE-12): add the shipped family's copyright line verbatim to static/fonts/OFL.txt and a row to static/fonts/LICENSES.md ('Copyright 2020 Braille Institute of America, Inc.' for the original family, or the Next family's own line if [USER] chooses Next there). Until CODE-12's precache change lands, every PWA install fetches all four faces (97,040 B), because the precache glob includes woff2; that cost is CODE-12's to settle.

**Acceptance test.** After the port onto v1.1: a network log of a page set in Atkinson shows only `/fonts/*` font requests; an explicit 'Ljóst' (the header toggle's title reads 'Ljóst þema') stays light with the OS in dark mode; under 'Fylgja kerfi', switching the OS colour scheme flips the theme without a stored value freezing it (this fails on v1.2 today); static/fonts/OFL.txt and LICENSES.md carry the Atkinson line; and a grep finds no 'Letur hannað fyrir lesblinda' in src (v1.1 :41 has it today).

**Not this (walked back in verification).**

- NOT: a stored theme always wins. On v1.2 a stored theme is not always a choice: the OS-derived theme is seeded on a first visit and saved at once, which freezes it. Keep the explicit-choice rule, not v1.2's implementation.
- NOT: the choice applies from the first paint. No theme script exists in app.html on any branch, so a stored dark theme paints light first.
- NOT: per-setting @font-face means a face downloads only when the setting is used. With the service worker, the woff2 precache glob makes every PWA install fetch all four faces (97,040 B) until the precache change lands.

**Source findings:** UI2-21
